          \left\Vert {\Dot{T}_g f_2(u)} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\theta}_{2,r}}\le C  \left\Vert {g} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{-\sigma}_{\infty,\infty}}\left\Vert {f_2(u)} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\theta+\sigma}_{2,r}}\le C\left\Vert {g} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{-\sigma+\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty}}\left\Vert {u} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\theta+\sigma}_{2,r}},
    \end{align*}
     gives \eqref{prod:2':p2}.
<!-- end proof-7 -->
<!-- propo-3: body_md -->
\label{est:X:s:pro}
   For all $i=1,2$, the following inequality holds
    \begin{equation}
    \label{pro:est:X:s}
     X_{p_i}\le  X_{{p_i},0}     +C(2^{-N_{0}}+ X_{p_2}+X_{p_2}X_{p_2})(X_{p_i}+X_{p_2}+X_{p_i}X_{p_2}).
    \end{equation}
<!-- end propo-3 -->
<!-- lem-9: body_md -->
For all $p_2\ge 2$ and $ s>-\min(1,\frac{d}{p_2})$. There exist two positive constants $c$ and $C$ such that, for all $t\in [0,T]$, the following inequality holds
    \begin{multline}
    \label{s:est:ly:l}
     \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l}+ c\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^{l} \le 2\left\Vert {V_0} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^{l}+ C\Bigl(\Vert \nabla V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l}+ \left\Vert {F^t} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}\\
    +\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\\
   + \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert { \nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\Bigr),       
    \end{multline}
with $F^t$ defined in \eqref{F_t}.
<!-- end lem-9 -->
<!-- proof-8: body_md -->
Starting from \eqref{01:est:ly:l}, using Condition (SK) combined with \eqref{eq:Lj:Vj} provides the existence of a positive constant $c_0$ such that

 \begin{equation}
    \label{1:est:ly:l:s}
    \frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{L}_j+  2^{2j}c_0\mathcal{L}_j \lesssim  \left\Vert \nabla V\right\Vert_{L^\infty}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_j}\left\Vert V_j\right\Vert_{L^2}
+ \sqrt{\mathcal{L}_j}\left\Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j F\right\Vert_{L^2}.
\end{equation}
Then, taking $ X=\mathcal{L}_j$, $p=2$ in the Lemma \ref{lem_der_int} guarantees that for all $t\in [0,T]$
\begin{equation}
    \label{2:est:ly:l:s}
    \sup_{0\le t\le T}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_j(t)}+  2^{2j}c_0\int^t_0\sqrt{\mathcal{L}_j} \lesssim \sqrt{\mathcal{L}_j(0)}+ \Vert \nabla V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^\infty)} \Vert V_j \Vert_{L^\infty_T(L^2)}\\
    + \Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j F \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}.
    \end{equation}

Now, we need to deal with the nonlinear terms $ F^V$ and $F^{V^2}$ defined in \eqref{F_t}. 
                                                     
                 
                       
                                       
            
From the estimate \eqref{prod:2':p2} of Lemma \ref{lem:prod} (we choose  $\theta=s$, $\sigma=1$) and H\"{o}lder inequality in time, we get 
\begin{multline}
    \label{est:lh:F}
   \Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j F^V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\\ \le
     Ck_j2^{-js}\left(\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\right),
\end{multline}
while taking  $\theta=s+1$ in \eqref{prod:2:p2} produces
\begin{multline}
    \label{est:lh:F2}
    \Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j F^{V^2} \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)} \le Ck_j2^{-js}\Bigl(\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla^2 V^2 } \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}\\
    + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\Bigr)\cdotp
    \end{multline}
                                                                                                
Plugging  this estimate into \eqref{2:est:ly:l:s}, and using \eqref{eq:Lj:Vj} combined with interpolation inequality in time  Lebesgue space yields for all $j\le N_{0}+1$
and $\gamma\in [1,\infty]$:
\begin{multline}
    \label{:3:s:est:ly:l}
    (c_0 2^{2j})^\frac{1}{\gamma}\Vert V_j \Vert_{L^\gamma_T(L^2)} \lesssim \left\Vert V_{0,j}\right\Vert_{L^2}+ \Vert \nabla V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^\infty)} \Vert V_j \Vert_{L^\infty_T(L^2)}+  \Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j F^{t} \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)} \\
    + k_j2^{-js}C\biggl( \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert\nabla V\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\\
   +\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert { \nabla^2 V^2)} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\biggr)\cdotp
\end{multline}

We get \eqref{s:est:ly:l} by multiplying \eqref{:3:s:est:ly:l} by $ 2^{js} $ and taking the $l^r$ norm ($j\le N_{0}+1$) of the previous inequality.
<!-- end proof-8 -->
<!-- lem-10: body_md -->
\label{lem:est:W:s}
   Let $p_2\ge 2$ and $ s>-\min(1,{d}/{p_2})$. There exist two positive constants $c$ and $C$ such that, for all $t\in [0,T]$, the following inequality holds
\begin{multline}
    \label{est:W:s}
    \left\Vert {W} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h+c   \left\Vert {W} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^h \le  2 \left\Vert {W_0} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^h+ 2^{-N_{0}}C   \left\Vert {\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+  \left\Vert {g^t} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})} \\
        +  C\biggl(\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\\
   + \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\biggr),
\end{multline}
   with $g^t$ defined in \eqref{def:g}.
<!-- end lem-10 -->
<!-- proof-9: body_md -->
Taking $p_i=2$  in  \eqref{est:p:W:1} leads to 
    \begin{align}
        \label{est:W:L2}
          \frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^d} \overline{S}^0_{22} W_j\cdot W_j+ c_0 2^{2j} \left\Vert W_j\right\Vert_{L^2}^2\le C \left\Vert W_j\right\Vert_{L^2}\left\Vert  \Dot{\Delta}_j g\right\Vert_{L^2},
    \end{align}
    where $g=g^V+g^{V^2}+g^t+\Bar{g}^V$ has been defined in \eqref{def:g}.
    
    Using  Lemma \ref{lem_der_int} with $p=2 $ and $X=\overline{S}^0_{22} W_j\cdot W_j,$ and remembering that the matrix $ \overline{S}^0_{22} $ is symmetric positive definite yields
\begin{align}
    \label{1:s:est:W} \Vert  W_j\Vert_{L^\infty_T(L^2)}+2^{2j}c_0   \Vert  W_j\Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\le C\left\Vert W_j(0)\right\Vert_{L^2}+  C\Vert  \Dot{\Delta}_j g \Vert_{L^\infty_T(L^2)}.
\end{align}
The terms $ \Dot{\Delta}_j g^V$ and $\Dot{\Delta}_j  g^{V^2}$ can be bounded in $L^1_T(L^2)$ exactly as 
for proving \eqref{s:est:ly:l}. Hence, from \eqref{est:lh:F} and \eqref{est:lh:F2} it holds:
\begin{align}
    \label{s:est:lh:g}
    \begin{split}
      \blacktriangleright&\Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j g^V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)} \\&\le
     Ck_j2^{-js}\left(\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\right),\\
      \blacktriangleright &\Vert \Dot{\Delta}_j g^{V^2} \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)} \\&\le 
      Ck_j2^{-js}\left(\Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla^2 V^2 } \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty})\right)\cdotp
     \end{split}
\end{align}
 Remembering the definition of $\Bar{g}^V$ (see \eqref{def:g}), using \eqref{bern:ine:M}  and bearing in mind that the homogeneous operators $ |D|^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{-1}(D)\mathcal{S}_{21}(D)$ and $|D|^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{-1}(D)\mathcal{S}_{22}(D)$ are of order $-1$, we get for all $j\ge N_{0}+2$.
 \begin{align*}
     &\Vert\Dot{\Delta}_j(\Bar{g}^V) \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}   \le C 2^{-j} \Vert\partial_t V \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}  .
 \end{align*}

We insert all this information into  \eqref{est:W:L2}, we then use interpolation inequalities in time  Lebesgue spaces (up to slight modification of $c_0$) and  we get for 
all $j\in \mathbb{Z}$ and $\gamma\in [1,\infty]$:
\begin{multline}
   \label{2:s:est:W}
  ( c_0 2^{2j})^\frac{1}{\gamma}\Vert  W_j\Vert_{L^\gamma_T(L^2)}\le   \left\Vert W_j(0)\right\Vert_{L^2}+ C2^{-(s+1)j}   k_j\left\Vert {\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h+\Vert  \Dot{\Delta}_j g^t\Vert_{L^\gamma_T(L^2)} \\
         + Ck_j2^{-js}\biggl( \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\\
   + \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert { \nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} \biggr)\cdotp
\end{multline}

To get \eqref{est:W:s}, we multiply \eqref{2:s:est:W} by $2^{js}$, then take its $l^r$ norm
on $j\geq N_0+2.$
<!-- end proof-9 -->
<!-- lem-11: body_md -->
\label{lem:est:V1:s}
   Let $p_2\ge 2$ and $ s>-\min(1,\frac{d}{p_2})$. We have for all $t\in [0,T]$:
\begin{multline}
    \label{est:V1:s}
     \left\Vert {V^1} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^h+c_0   \left\Vert {V^1} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^h \le   \left\Vert {V^1_0} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^h+ 2^{-N_{0}}   \left\Vert { V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^h+ \left\Vert { W} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^h\\
    Ck_j2^{-j(s+1)}\biggl(  \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert  \nabla V^1\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} +\lVert  \nabla V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {V^1} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\\
     +  \lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert { \nabla V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}  + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert   \nabla V^2\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})}\biggr)\cdotp
\end{multline}
<!-- end lem-11 -->
<!-- proof-10: body_md -->
The proof of Lemma \ref{lem:est:V1:s} follows the lines of Lemma \ref{est:V1:hf:lem}. The difference lies on estimating the source term in  $L^1(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})$ instead  of $ L^1(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_i}}+1}_{p_i,1})$, with $i=1,2$. 
    Starting from \eqref{est:V1:ini} (where we take $p_i=2$) and using Lemma \ref{lem_der_int} with $p=2$ yields for all $j\in \mathbb{Z}$:
     \begin{equation}
     \label{est:z:s:hf}
    \Vert  z_j\Vert_{L^\infty_T(L^2)}+c_0   \Vert  z_j\Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\le \left\Vert z_j(0)\right\Vert_{L^2}+ C \Vert  ( \Dot{\Delta}_j h ,R_j)\Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\\
       +C\int^T_0 \left\Vert \nabla H(U)\right\Vert_{L^\infty}\left\Vert {2}\right\Vert_{L^{z_j}},
       \end{equation}
where we recall $h= h^W+h^{V^2}_0+h^{V^2}_1  $ (see \eqref{def:ht:V:V2}) and 
\begin{align}
     \label{def:Rj:recal}
    R_j=\sum_{\alpha=1}^d \left[\Dot{\Delta}_j,H^\alpha(U)- H^\alpha(\overline{U})\right]\partial_\alpha z.
 \end{align}
 Choosing $p=2, \Tilde{p}=p_2$ in \eqref{comm:est:1} and applying the outcome result to $R_j$, since $s+1>-\frac{d}{p_2}$ and $s>-1$, we have
 \begin{multline}
 \label{est:Rj:s:hf}
       \Vert  R_j \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\\
       \le C2^{-(s+1)j}k_j\left( \lVert \nabla V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {z} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}+  \lVert\nabla z \rVert_{L^{2}(L^\infty)}  \left\Vert { V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\right)\cdotp
                                                                                                                                                                                                               
\end{multline}
From Bernstein's inequality we have
\begin{align}
\label{est:hz:s:hf}
      \Vert  (\Dot{\Delta}_j h^W, \Dot{\Delta}_j h^{V^2}_0) \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\le C  \Vert  (2^j \Dot{\Delta}_j W, \Dot{\Delta}_j V^2) \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}.
\end{align}
The term $ h^{V^2}_{1}$ can be treated using Lemma \ref{lem:prod}  combined with Proposition \ref{propo:comp} . As we are working under the assumption that $s>-\frac{d}{p_2}$, applying \eqref{prod:2':p2} yields
\begin{multline}
\label{est:hW:s:hf}
       \Vert  \Dot{\Delta}_j h^{V^2}_{1} \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\le Ck_j2^{-j(s+1)}\biggl(\lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {\nabla V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\\
    + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\lVert \nabla V^2\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})}\biggr)\cdotp
\end{multline}
Now, inserting \eqref{est:hz:s:hf}, \eqref{est:hW:s:hf} and \eqref{est:Rj:s:hf}   into \eqref{est:z:s:hf} and using interpolation inequalities in time Lebesgue spaces yields for all $j\ge N_{0}+2$ and $\gamma\in [1,\infty]$:
  \begin{multline}
   \label{2:s:est:v1}
   ( c_0 2^{2j})^\frac{1}{\gamma}\Vert  z\Vert_{L^\gamma_T(L^2)}\le   \left\Vert z_j(0)\right\Vert_{L^2}+C\int^T_0 \left\Vert \nabla V\right\Vert_{L^\infty}\left\Vert {2}\right\Vert_{L^{z_j}}+ C  \Vert  (\Dot{\Delta}_j V^2,2^j\Dot{\Delta}_j W) \Vert_{L^1_T(L^2)}\\
    + Ck_j2^{-j(s+1)}\biggl( \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})} \Vert   \nabla z\Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}+\lVert  \nabla V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)} \left\Vert {z} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\\
     + \lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert { \nabla V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}  + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert   \nabla V^2 \Vert_{L^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})}\biggr)\cdotp
  \end{multline}


We get the desired estimate after summing \eqref{2:s:est:v1} over $j\ge N_{0}+2$ and remarking that any Lebesgue norm of $\Dot{\Delta}_j z$ is equivalent to $ \Dot{\Delta}_j V^1$ (hence $\left\Vert {z} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{p,r}}\simeq \left\Vert {V^1} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{p,r}}$).
<!-- end proof-10 -->
<!-- lem-12: body_md -->
\label{lem:est:pt:V:s}
Let $k:\mathbb{R}^n\mapsto \mathbb{R}$ be  a regular function satisfying $k(\overline{U})=0$.
It holds
    \begin{align}
        \label{est:ptV:s:h}
        \left\Vert {k(U)\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h\lesssim X_{p_2}+(X_{p_2})^2.
    \end{align}
<!-- end lem-12 -->
<!-- proof-11: body_md -->
The proof of Lemma \ref{lem:est:pt:V:s} will be based on Lemma \ref{lem:prod}.  Let us remark that, since $k(\overline{U}) =0$, then  $k(U)\partial_t V$ satisfies the following equation
  \begin{multline}
      \label{eq:pt V:s}
  k(U) \partial_t V = \sum_{\alpha=1}^d \left( k(U)(S^0(U))^{-1} S^\alpha(U)- k(\overline{U})(\overline{S^0})^{-1}\overline{S^\alpha}\right) \partial_\alpha V \\
       + \sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d \left( k(U)(S^0(U))^{-1} Y^{\alpha\beta}(U)-k(\overline{U})(\overline{S^0})^{-1} \overline{Y}^{\alpha\beta}\right)\partial^2_{\alpha\beta} V\\+  k(U)(S^0(U))^{-1} \sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d \partial_\alpha (Y^{\alpha\beta}(V))\partial_\beta V.
         \end{multline}
 First, using \eqref{prod:2:p2} and \eqref{smal:cnd:Vs} combined with                                                                                                                    
 Proposition \ref{propo:comp} and the form of each matrix $Y^{\alpha\beta}(U)$ we get for  $s>-\min(1,\frac{d}{{p_2}})$
  \begin{multline*}
       \left\Vert {  k(U)(S^0(U))^{-1}\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d \partial_\alpha (Y^{\alpha\beta}(V))\partial_\beta V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})} \\
      \lesssim \lVert \nabla V\rVert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}
      + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V^2 \Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}
  \end{multline*}
 while using \eqref{prod:2':p2} with $\theta=s, \sigma=1$ we have for all $\alpha,\beta=1,\cdots, d$
  \begin{multline*}
      \left\Vert {(k(U)(S^0(U))^{-1} Y^{\alpha\beta}(U)-k(\overline{U})(\overline{S^0})^{-1} \overline{Y}^{\alpha\beta})\partial^2_{\alpha\beta}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}\\\lesssim 
     \left\Vert { \nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h+  \lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L_T^{\infty}(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla^2 V^2 \Vert_{L^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}.
  \end{multline*}
  Finally, taking again $\theta=s, \sigma=1$  in \eqref{prod:2':p2} of Lemma \ref{lem:prod} for the first   middle term on the right-hand side of \eqref{eq:pt V:s},  we get for all $s>-\min(\frac{d}{p_2},1)$,
  \begin{multline}
  \label{est:ptV:hf:s:fin}
    \left\Vert {k(U)\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h\lesssim 
      \lVert \nabla V\rVert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V^2 \Vert_{L^{2}_T(L^\infty)}\\
       +\lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\rho_1}(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}  + \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}\\
       +\lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{\infty}(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla^2 V^2} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla^2 V^2 \Vert_{L^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})}.
  \end{multline}
   The first two terms on the right-hand side of the above are bounded by $(X_{p_2})^2$.\\
  Note that, the use of basic Besov embeddings \eqref{link:lf:hf} and \eqref{pi:s:r:d} provides
\begin{align}
    \label{es:inf:X}
    \left\Vert f\right\Vert_{L^\infty}\lesssim \left\Vert {f^{l}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}}_{2,1}}+\left\Vert {f^h} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}\lesssim \left\Vert {f} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^{l}+\left\Vert {f} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}. 
\end{align}
From \eqref{est:lh:hf}, \eqref{es:inf:X} and \eqref{link:CL:Besov:space:chap2} that shows the relationship between the Besov space $L^\rho_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{p,r})$ and Chemin-Lerner-Besov space $ \widetilde{L}^\rho(\dot{B}^{s}_{p,r})$,
we have
\begin{align}\label{est:st:s:1}
   \lVert  V\rVert_{\Tilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})\cap L^{2}(L^\infty)}\left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})} &\lesssim   \left(\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^{l}+\lVert V\rVert^h_{L^2_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty}\cap L^\infty)}\right) \left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l}  \nonumber\\\nonumber
   &+  \left(\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l}+ \lVert V\rVert^h_{L^\infty_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty}\cap L^\infty)}\right) \left\Vert {\nabla V} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^h\\
   &\lesssim  (X_{p_2})^2,
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{est:st:s:2}
   \Vert V \Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}\Vert \nabla V\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}-1}_{p_2,\infty})} &\lesssim   \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}(\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,\infty})}^{l}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{L^{2}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,1})}^h)
   \nonumber\\\nonumber
     &\lesssim   \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}(\left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{2}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l}+ \left\Vert {V} \right\Vert_{L^{2}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{{p_2}}}_{p_2,1})}^h)\\
   &\lesssim  (X_{p_2})^2.
   \end{align}

Similarly, we can prove that the remaining terms of \eqref{est:ptV:hf:s:fin} are bounded by $(X_{p_2})^2$, which finishes the proof of Lemma \ref{lem:est:pt:V:s}.
<!-- end proof-11 -->
<!-- head-1: body_md -->
\begin{description}
    \item[(G1)] There exists an entropy function $s=s(\rho,e)$
       such that the following first law of thermodynamics is satisfied: 
    \begin{align}
    \label{Gibs}
    Tds=de+pd(\frac{1}{\rho})\cdotp
\end{align}
    Furthermore we assume that: for all $\rho>0$ and $\theta>0$,
    \begin{align}
        \label{positive:Pr:eth}
        p_\rho(\rho,\theta) =\frac{\partial p}{\partial\rho}>0 \quad \text{and} \quad e_\theta(\rho,\theta)= \frac{\partial e}{\partial\theta}>0.
    \end{align}
    \item[(G2)] The viscosities $\mu$, $\lambda$ and the heat conductivity $k$ are smooth functions of $\rho>0$ and $\theta,$ and satisfy 
    \begin{align}
        \label{LAme:cnd}
       \mu>0,\quad \text{ } \quad \nu\overset{\rm def}{=} 2\mu+\lambda>0 \quad \text{and} \quad k>0.
    \end{align}
\end{description}
It is important to emphasize that the condition $  2\mu + \lambda > 0 $ is stronger than the one usually used, namely $  2\mu + d\lambda > 0 $, which we actually adopt in \cite{DanADOglob}. Indeed, since $\mu > 0$, it is clear that $2\mu > -d\lambda$ implies $2\mu > -\lambda$.
\smallbreak
In order to apply the above results to the system \eqref{CFNS}, we need to rewrite it as partially symmetric hyperbolic diffuse system. For that purpose, we set
\begin{align*}
    \mathcal{U}\overset{\rm def}{=} \{(\rho,u,\theta)\in 
    \mathbb R\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb R/ \rho>0,\; \theta>0\}.
\end{align*}
For some positive constants $\overline{\rho}>0$ and $\overline{\theta}>0$, define 
\begin{align*}
    U\overset{\rm def}{=}(\rho,u,\theta) ,\;\; \overline{U}\overset{\rm def}{=} (\overline{\rho},0_{\mathbb{R}^d},\overline{\theta}) \quad \text{and} \quad V\overset{\rm def}{=} U-\overline{U}.
\end{align*}
Then, \eqref{CFNS} is reduced to
\begin{align}
    \label{SHPDS:NS}
    S^0(U)\frac{d}{dt} U+\sum_{\alpha=1}^d S^\alpha(U)\partial_\alpha U-\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d \partial_\alpha\left( Y^{\alpha\beta}\partial_\beta V\right)=H(U)
\end{align}
where
\begin{align}
    \label{def:S0:H:xi}
    S^0(U)\overset{\rm def}{=} 
    \begin{pmatrix}
\frac{p_\rho}{\rho} & 0 & 0\\
0 & \rho Id & 0\\
0&0 & \frac{\rho e_\theta}{\theta}
\end{pmatrix},\;\;\;
 H(U)\overset{\rm def}{=}  \begin{pmatrix}
0\\
0 \\
\Psi-k\nabla \theta\cdot \nabla (\frac{1}{\theta})
\end{pmatrix},
\end{align}

\begin{align}
    \label{def:Salpha:}
\sum_{\alpha=1}^d S^\alpha(U)\xi_\alpha \overset{\rm def}{=}
 \begin{pmatrix}
\frac{p_\rho}{\rho}u\cdot \xi & p_\rho \xi & 0\\
p_\rho ^T\xi & \rho(u\cdot \xi) Id & p_\theta ^T\xi\\
0& p_\theta \xi & \frac{\rho e_\theta}{\theta}u\cdot \xi
\end{pmatrix},
\end{align}
and
\begin{align}
    \label{Yalbe:xi}
    Y^{\alpha\beta} \overset{\rm def}{=}
    \begin{pmatrix}
0& 0 \\
0 & Z^{\alpha\beta}
\end{pmatrix} 
\end{align}
\text{  with  }
\begin{align}
\label{def:Zal:be:xi}
\sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d Z^{\alpha\beta}\xi_\alpha\zeta_\beta\overset{\rm def}{=} 
\begin{pmatrix}
 \mu \xi\cdot \zeta{\rm Id}+(\mu+\lambda)\xi\otimes \zeta   & 0\\
0 & \frac{k}{\theta} \xi\cdot \zeta
\end{pmatrix},\quad \xi,\zeta\in \mathbb{R}^d\cdotp
\end{align}

We recall that
\begin{align*}
    \xi\cdot \zeta=\sum_{\alpha=1}^d \xi_\alpha\zeta_\alpha \cdotp
\end{align*}

We observe that $S^0(U)$ is a (real) diagonal positive definite matrix for all $U\in \mathcal{U}$, and that the matrices $S^\alpha(U)$ are real symmetric. Furthermore a simple calculation shows that: for all $X,\xi\in \mathbb{R}^d$,  $Y\in \mathbb{R}$, 
\begin{align}
    \label{sca:Yal:be:V}
     \sum_{\alpha,\beta=1}^d \left\langle Z^{\alpha\beta}\xi_\alpha\xi_\beta A, A\right\rangle\ge \min(\mu,\nu)|X|^2+\frac{k}{\theta}Y^2
\end{align}
where $A\overset{\rm def}{=} (X,Y)$ and $\left\langle \cdot ,\cdot \right\rangle$ denotes the standard inner product in $\mathbb{R}^{d+1}$. As for the right-hand side~$H,$ it is regarded as a lower order quadratic term. 

It is obvious that System \eqref{CFNS} under assumption  \textbf{G} satisfies hypotheses  (\textbf{H1})-(\textbf{H3}). As $n_1=1$, Assumption  (\textbf{H4}) is clearly satisfied and we can choose $P$ equal to the constant $1$. Therefore   (\textbf{H5}) is verified. Moreover, if we  set
$$B:\omega\in \mathbb{S}^{d-1}\mapsto B(\omega)=\overline{\rho}P_{\rho}(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})^2 \omega N^{-1}(\omega) {}^T\omega \quad \text{with} \quad N(\omega)\overset{\rm def}{=} \overline{\mu}{\rm Id} +(\overline{\mu}+\overline{\lambda})\omega\otimes\omega, $$
and $ \overline{\mu}\overset{\rm def}{=} \mu(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta}),  \overline{\lambda}\overset{\rm def}{=} \lambda(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})  $ then, denoting $\overline{\nu}\overset{\rm def}{=} \nu(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})$, we can verify that:
$$ N^{-1}(\omega)= \overline{\mu}^{-1}{\rm Id}+ (\overline{\nu}^{-1}-\overline{\mu}^{-1})\omega\otimes\omega.$$


Thus, for all $\omega\in \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$,
\begin{align}
    B(\omega)= \overline{\rho}P_{\rho}(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})^2 \overline{\nu}^{-1}.
\end{align}
Hence $B$ is independent of $\omega$ and  under assumption \textbf{G}, $B>0$. So assumption (\textbf{H6}) is fulfilled. The vector $H $ defined in \eqref{def:S0:H:xi} verifies the assumption (\textbf{H7}). Here the (SK) condition is equivalent to $P_\rho(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})\neq 0$, which is satisfied, under Assumption \textbf{G}.
\smallbreak
We can thus apply Theorem \ref{thm:p} to System \eqref{CFNS}, getting:
\begin{thm}    \label{thm:glo:CFNS:p}
   Let $d\ge 2$, $ 2\le  p_1\le p_2< 2d $ and $p_2\le \min( 4,\frac{2d}{d-2})$.  Let assumption \textbf{G} be in force.  Assume that the initial data $(\rho_0,u_0,\theta_0)$ are in $\mathcal{U}$ and set $V^1_0=\rho_0-\overline{\rho}$, $V^2_0=(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})$ and $V_0=(V^1_0,V^2_0)$. 

     Then, there exists an integer $N_0$ depending only on the coefficients of the system
     and on $\bar\rho,$      
     and a positive constant $c_0$ such that if   $ (\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})^{l,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1} $, $ (\rho_0-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}}_{p_1,1}$ and $ (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}-1}_{p_1,1}$ 
    and
    \[   \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1}}^{l,N_0} +\left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0} \le c_0, \]
    then \eqref{CFNS} has a unique global-in-time solution $(\rho,u,\theta)\in \mathcal{U} $. Moreover  $V\overset{\rm def}{=}(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})$ in the space  $\mathbb{E}_{p_1}\cap \mathbb{E}_{p_2}$ with $\mathbb{E}_{p_i}$ defined by
    \begin{multline*}
           (\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{l,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}+1}_{2,1}); \;\;  (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1}) \\
             (u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}, W^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1});\;\; (\partial_t\rho, \partial_t u, \partial_t \theta)\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1}) 
    \end{multline*}
with 
\[ W= i|D|^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{-1}(D)\left( \mathcal{S}_{21}(D) (\rho-\overline{\rho})+  \mathcal{S}_{22}(D)\begin{pmatrix}
    u\\
    \theta-\overline{\theta}
\end{pmatrix}\right)  +\begin{pmatrix}
    u\\
    \theta-\overline{\theta}
\end{pmatrix}\]
and
$$\displaylines{
                
 \mathcal{S}_{21}(D)=
    \begin{pmatrix}
        & \frac{P(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})^T\nabla}{|D|}\\
        &0
    \end{pmatrix},\;
    \Delta\mathcal{Z}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}
       & -\overline{\mu}\Delta {\rm Id} + (\overline{\lambda}+\overline{\mu}\nabla{\rm div}) & 0\\
        & 0 & -\frac{k(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})}{\overline{\theta}}
    \end{pmatrix},\;\cr
    \mathcal{S}_{22}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}
        &0 & P_\theta(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})\frac{^T \nabla}{|D|}\\
       & P_\theta(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})\frac{ \nabla}{|D|} & 0
    \end{pmatrix}\cdotp}$$

Furthermore we have the following bounds: 
\begin{align*}
    \mathbb{V}_{p_i}&\lesssim  \mathbb{V}_{{p_i},0}\overset{\rm def}{=}  \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1}}^{l,N_0} +\left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}
\end{align*}
with  for $1\le i\le 2$
\begin{multline*}
      \mathbb{V}_{p_i} = \left\Vert {(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1})}^{l,N_0}+\left\Vert {(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}+1}_{2,1})}^{l,N_0} + \Vert \rho-\overline{\rho}\Vert_{L^\infty\cap L^1(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}\\
    + 
    \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0} + \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}+  \left\Vert {\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}.
\end{multline*}
    
    
\end{thm}

Applying Theorem \ref{thm:p:s} gives us

\begin{thm}
    \label{thm:glo:CFNS:p:s}
    Let $d\ge 2$. Let Assumption \textbf{G} be in force.   Assume that $(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}+1}_{p_1,1}$, $ (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}}_{p_1,1} $ for some $p_1\ge 2$ with $\rho_0,u_0,\theta_0\in \mathcal{U}$. Let $p_2\ge p_1$. Then there exists an integer $N_0$ 
    depending only on the coefficients of the system and on $\bar\rho,$ such that if we make the following additional  (lower order) assumptions 
    \[  (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta}), (\rho_0-\overline{\rho})^{l,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{s}_{2,r} ;\;\; 
    (\rho_0^{h,N_0}-\bar\rho)\in   \dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r} \]
    for some $r\in [1,\infty]$ and $s$ such that
    \begin{align}
        \label{def:s:r:p2:CFNS}
         s<\frac{d}{2} \;\; \text{and}\;\; r>1 \quad \text{or} \quad s=\frac{d}{2}-1 \;\; \text{and}\;\; r=1,
    \end{align}
     then there exists a  positive constant $c_0$ such that if  
   \begin{align*}
       \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}+  \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^{h,N_0} +\left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}\le  c_0,
   \end{align*}
    then \eqref{CFNS} has a global-in-time solution $(\rho,u,\theta)\in \mathcal{U}$. Moreover  $V\overset{\rm def}{=}(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})$ in the space $\mathfrak{E}_{p_1}^s\cap \mathfrak{E}_{p_2}^s$ with 
    $\mathfrak{E}_{p_i}^s$ defined by
     \begin{align*}
          &   (u,\theta-\overline{\theta}), (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{l,N_0}\in \widetilde{\mathcal{C}}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r}); \;\; (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{h,N_0}\in \widetilde{\mathcal{C}}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})\cap \widetilde L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})\\
            &  (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1}) ;\;\; (\partial_t\rho,\partial_t u,\partial_t \theta)\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})  \\
            & (u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}, W^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1}),
    \end{align*}
with $W$ defined in Theorem \ref{thm:glo:CFNS:p} .
Next, we have the following bound for all $t\ge 0$:
\begin{multline*}
  X_{p_i}(t)\lesssim  
     \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}+  \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^{h,N_0} +\left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}
\end{multline*}
with 
\begin{multline*}
     X_{p_i}(T) \overset{\rm def}{=} \left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {(u,\theta-\overline{\theta}) } \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^{l,N_0}\\
    +\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^{h,N_0}+\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^{h,N_0}+ \Vert \rho-\overline{\rho}\Vert_{L^\infty\cap L^1(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})}\\
    + 
    \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0} + \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}.
\end{multline*}

In addition, the uniqueness holds for $p_2<2d$.

\medbreak
<!-- end head-1 -->
<!-- thm-3: body_md -->
\label{thm:glo:CFNS:p}
   Let $d\ge 2$, $ 2\le  p_1\le p_2< 2d $ and $p_2\le \min( 4,\frac{2d}{d-2})$.  Let assumption \textbf{G} be in force.  Assume that the initial data $(\rho_0,u_0,\theta_0)$ are in $\mathcal{U}$ and set $V^1_0=\rho_0-\overline{\rho}$, $V^2_0=(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})$ and $V_0=(V^1_0,V^2_0)$. 

     Then, there exists an integer $N_0$ depending only on the coefficients of the system
     and on $\bar\rho,$      
     and a positive constant $c_0$ such that if   $ (\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})^{l,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1} $, $ (\rho_0-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}}_{p_1,1}$ and $ (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}-1}_{p_1,1}$ 
    and
    \[   \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1}}^{l,N_0} +\left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0} \le c_0, \]
    then \eqref{CFNS} has a unique global-in-time solution $(\rho,u,\theta)\in \mathcal{U} $. Moreover  $V\overset{\rm def}{=}(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})$ in the space  $\mathbb{E}_{p_1}\cap \mathbb{E}_{p_2}$ with $\mathbb{E}_{p_i}$ defined by
    \begin{multline*}
           (\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{l,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}+1}_{2,1}); \;\;  (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+\!,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1}) \\
             (u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}, W^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1});\;\; (\partial_t\rho, \partial_t u, \partial_t \theta)\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1}) 
    \end{multline*}
with 
\[ W= i|D|^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{-1}(D)\left( \mathcal{S}_{21}(D) (\rho-\overline{\rho})+  \mathcal{S}_{22}(D)\begin{pmatrix}
    u\\
    \theta-\overline{\theta}
\end{pmatrix}\right)  +\begin{pmatrix}
    u\\
    \theta-\overline{\theta}
\end{pmatrix}\]
and
$$\displaylines{
                
 \mathcal{S}_{21}(D)=
    \begin{pmatrix}
        & \frac{P(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})^T\nabla}{|D|}\\
        &0
    \end{pmatrix},\;
    \Delta\mathcal{Z}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}
       & -\overline{\mu}\Delta {\rm Id} + (\overline{\lambda}+\overline{\mu}\nabla{\rm div}) & 0\\
        & 0 & -\frac{k(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})}{\overline{\theta}}
    \end{pmatrix},\;\cr
    \mathcal{S}_{22}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}
        &0 & P_\theta(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})\frac{^T \nabla}{|D|}\\
       & P_\theta(\overline{\rho},\overline{\theta})\frac{ \nabla}{|D|} & 0
    \end{pmatrix}\cdotp}$$

Furthermore we have the following bounds: 
\begin{align*}
    \mathbb{V}_{p_i}&\lesssim  \mathbb{V}_{{p_i},0}\overset{\rm def}{=}  \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho},u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1}}^{l,N_0} +\left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}
\end{align*}
with  for $1\le i\le 2$
\begin{multline*}
      \mathbb{V}_{p_i} = \left\Vert {(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}-1}_{2,1})}^{l,N_0}+\left\Vert {(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{2}+1}_{2,1})}^{l,N_0} + \Vert \rho-\overline{\rho}\Vert_{L^\infty\cap L^1(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}\\
    + 
    \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0} + \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}+  \left\Vert {\partial_t V} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}.
\end{multline*}
<!-- end thm-3 -->
<!-- thm-4: body_md -->
\label{thm:glo:CFNS:p:s}
    Let $d\ge 2$. Let Assumption \textbf{G} be in force.   Assume that $(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}+1}_{p_1,1}$, $ (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})\in \dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_1}}_{p_1,1} $ for some $p_1\ge 2$ with $\rho_0,u_0,\theta_0\in \mathcal{U}$. Let $p_2\ge p_1$. Then there exists an integer $N_0$ 
    depending only on the coefficients of the system and on $\bar\rho,$ such that if we make the following additional  (lower order) assumptions 
    \[  (u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta}), (\rho_0-\overline{\rho})^{l,N_0}\in \dot{B}^{s}_{2,r} ;\;\; 
    (\rho_0^{h,N_0}-\bar\rho)\in   \dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r} \]
    for some $r\in [1,\infty]$ and $s$ such that
    \begin{align}
        \label{def:s:r:p2:CFNS}
         s<\frac{d}{2} \;\; \text{and}\;\; r>1 \quad \text{or} \quad s=\frac{d}{2}-1 \;\; \text{and}\;\; r=1,
    \end{align}
     then there exists a  positive constant $c_0$ such that if  
   \begin{align*}
       \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}+  \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^{h,N_0} +\left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(\rho_0-\overline{\rho})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}\le  c_0,
   \end{align*}
    then \eqref{CFNS} has a global-in-time solution $(\rho,u,\theta)\in \mathcal{U}$. Moreover  $V\overset{\rm def}{=}(\rho-\overline{\rho},u,\theta-\overline{\theta})$ in the space $\mathfrak{E}_{p_1}^s\cap \mathfrak{E}_{p_2}^s$ with 
    $\mathfrak{E}_{p_i}^s$ defined by
     \begin{align*}
          &   (u,\theta-\overline{\theta}), (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{l,N_0}\in \widetilde{\mathcal{C}}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r}); \;\; (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{h,N_0}\in \widetilde{\mathcal{C}}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})\cap \widetilde L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})\\
            &  (\rho-\overline{\rho})^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1}) ;\;\; (\partial_t\rho,\partial_t u,\partial_t \theta)\in L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})  \\
            & (u,\theta-\overline{\theta})^{H,N_0}, W^{H,N_0}\in \mathcal{C}_b(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})\cap L^1(\mathbb{R}^+,\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1}),
    \end{align*}
with $W$ defined in Theorem \ref{thm:glo:CFNS:p} .
Next, we have the following bound for all $t\ge 0$:
\begin{multline*}
  X_{p_i}(t)\lesssim  
     \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r}}+  \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r}}^{h,N_0} +\left\Vert {(u_0,\theta_0-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}+ \left\Vert {\rho_0-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_2}+1}_{p_2,1}}^{h,N_0}
\end{multline*}
with 
\begin{multline*}
     X_{p_i}(T) \overset{\rm def}{=} \left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}^{l,N_0}+ \left\Vert {(u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {(u,\theta-\overline{\theta}) } \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}+\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+2}_{2,r})}^{l,N_0}\\
    +\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{\infty}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^{h,N_0}+\left\Vert {\rho-\overline{\rho}} \right\Vert_{\widetilde{L}^{1}_T(\dot{B}^{s+1}_{2,r})}^{h,N_0}+ \Vert \rho-\overline{\rho}\Vert_{L^\infty\cap L^1(\dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+1}_{p_i,1})}\\
    + 
    \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{\infty}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0} + \left\Vert {(W,u,\theta-\overline{\theta})} \right\Vert_{L^{1}_T(\Dot{B}^{\frac{d}{p_i}+2}_{p_i,1})}^{h,N_0}.
\end{multline*}

In addition, the uniqueness holds for $p_2<2d$.
